Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on rooms structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 829. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 286 with relations in degree 4.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_829(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 829. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^286(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 214.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Every structural decomposition deformation of the rooms object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 552. The moduli space has dimension at most 663 with 4 connected components.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 552 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the rooms structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 663. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
